- **vet-idea**: Vet a product, app, business, or feature idea against the real market before building it — scope the concept, survey competitors (paid and open source), test commercial viability, clear every source and dependency for licensing and compliance risk, then land a short, memorable name plus positioning and messaging. Use whenever the user wants to plan a new product or app, asks "does this already exist", "is this a good idea", "who else is doing this", "can this be monetized", "what am I missing", "is this name taken", "what should we call it", wants a market assessment, competitive landscape, e… ([jgobuilds/ai-standards-public](https://github.com/jgobuilds/ai-standards-public/tree/HEAD/skills/vet-idea))
- **vet-idea**: Vets an idea, decision, or plan BEFORE it is built. Runs a single model through five fixed adversarial advisor roles, a peer-review pass, and a chairman synthesis that emits a halt-capable GO / NO-GO / RECONSIDER verdict. Advisory and non-mutating — outputs a report, never writes task state. Use when an idea, feature, or costly hard-to-reverse decision is of uncertain worth and the question is whether to build it at all. NOT FOR settled work (bug fixes, approved features) or for shaping how to build it (the ISA Interview workflow). ([rynhardt81/forgeFlow](https://github.com/rynhardt81/forgeFlow/tree/HEAD/skills/vet-idea))
- **vet-install**: Trust assessment before installing, fixing, integrating, or debugging third-party software — any `curl | sh` installer, root install, closed binary, a plugin or launcher that downloads a prebuilt binary (build it from source instead), browser extension, npm/pip/distro package from an unfamiliar vendor, VPN/DNS/proxy service, or anything touching DNS, network, auth, or the kernel. - **vflow-check**: 内容流水线第 5 阶段·发布前检查。分六层过：平台机器会识别什么（限流/审核风险）、内容本身有什么实质问题（事实、承诺、误导）、AI 味（机器化表达招限流也让观众出戏）、声明台账（数字要有来源和口径）、画面层（承载体兑现、只有字的页、字幕安全区、最小字号），再加封面与成片的机器质检。用户要给一条已成形的内容做发布前检查、查敏感词、查风险、查 AI 痕迹时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/05-check.md，不落文件等于没做完。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-check))
- **vflow-core-rules**: Rules every pipeline agent follows. Preloaded by agents; not for direct use. ([KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow](https://github.com/KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/vflow-core-rules))
- **vflow-demo**: Runs the AXI demo end to end with sealed seeded bugs. Use when the user types /vflow-demo. ([KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow](https://github.com/KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/vflow-demo))
- **vflow-outline**: 内容结构框架阶段。先选六个结构模板之一，再把选题卡排成页面级/段落级结构——每页一个点、标注四个大刺激点和中段 re-hook、标好可保存物出现的位置。用户要搭框架、排结构、分大纲、安排论证顺序时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/02-outline.md。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-outline))
- **vflow-package**: 内容流水线第 4 阶段·包装。生成文字标题、封面标题、开头 3 秒三件套，按平台各出一版；小红书要做关键词矩阵（核心词/长尾词/问题词/场景词），并检查正文有没有兑现开头承诺。用户要起标题、写封面字、设计开头钩子、改开头、多平台适配、铺搜索词时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/04-package.md。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-package))
- **vflow-produce**: 内容制作阶段。把已定稿的口播稿做成成片——路线 A 用 HTML 幻灯片 + TTS + 录屏自动出片，路线 B 白板手绘动画，路线 C 真人出镜提词稿。也用于重做某一步（重录、重混 BGM、重烧字幕、换音色、加封面）。用户要生成视频、出成片、渲染、重录、加字幕封面时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/output/ 与 publish-copy.md。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-produce))
- **vflow-quick**: Runs a small slice of the pipeline end to end, to prove the flow works. Use when the user types /vflow-quick &lt;block&gt;. ([KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow](https://github.com/KamonHuiz/VLSI-Verification-Full-Flow/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/vflow-quick))
- **vflow-review**: 内容流水线第 7 阶段·复盘。回填 48 小时数据（播放、完播、互动、涨粉），判断哪一步做对了、下一条改什么。观察先进证据台账，攒够三条可比证据才准升级为默认。用户要复盘、看数据、总结规律、分析为什么这条没数据时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/06-review.md。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-review))
- **vflow-script**: 内容流水线第 3 阶段·剧本。先选结构模板（六个），再写念出来不卡的口播稿：钩子三变体、双轨时长（抖音发现轨 / 小红书深度轨）、四个大刺激点、可保存物、中段 re-hook。也用于检查已有稿子的留存和流畅度。用户要写剧本、口播稿、逐字稿、改稿、查留存、查流畅度时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/03-script.md。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-script))

- **vflow-topic**: 内容流水线第 1 阶段·选题。把模糊想法变成一张可判断、可放弃的选题卡——回答讲什么、给谁看、凭什么有人看完、和同类有什么不一样。用户要开新选题、判断一个想法值不值得做、找对标、分析观众动机时使用。产出 projects/&lt;slug&gt;/01-topic.md，不落文件等于没做完。 ([weikestudio/Vflow](https://github.com/weikestudio/Vflow/tree/HEAD/skills/vflow-topic))
